[codex] ## Operating Rules
[codex] 
[codex] ### Repository and Git Safety
[codex] 
[codex] - Never run git write operations such as `commit`, `push`, `checkout`, `reset`,
[codex]   branch manipulation, or history rewriting unless the user explicitly asks.
[codex] - Do not write outside this repository. Surface required sibling-repository
[codex]   changes instead of making them.
[codex] - Stop and report a missing prerequisite or an authority-boundary conflict. Do
[codex]   not invent a workaround.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Java and Spring Architecture
[codex] 
[codex] - Follow the shared layered architecture: controllers own HTTP concerns,
[codex]   services own business rules and transactions, repositories own data access,
[codex]   and entities carry persistence state.
[codex] - Use `AuditableEntity` and `SoftDeletableEntity` according to the shared entity
[codex]   decision rules. Keep pure association rows simple when row existence is the
[codex]   business fact.
[codex] - Use the shared `ServletApiExceptionHandler` and exception hierarchy for
[codex]   consistent error responses.
[codex] - Name API models `*Request` and `*Response`; never use `*Dto` or `*DTO`.
[codex] - Use provider-independent identifiers in `{prefix}_{full-uuid-hex}` form.
[codex] - Import persistence APIs from `jakarta.persistence.*`; never use
[codex]   `org.hibernate.*` APIs.
[codex] - Apply the Java quality rules for `var`, full variable names, explicit imports,
[codex]   and Javadoc punctuation from the required code-quality document.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Architectural Simplicity
[codex] 
[codex] - Choose the simplest implementation that correctly handles realistic inputs,
[codex]   states, and failure modes. Do not trade away security, data integrity, or
[codex]   required behavior for brevity.
[codex] - Put validation in the layer that owns the rule: request models and
[codex]   controllers validate request shape and syntax; services validate business
[codex]   invariants, ownership, persistence state, and cross-entity rules.
[codex] - Do not duplicate API validation in the service layer when every caller passes
[codex]   through the validated API contract. Validate again only when another caller
[codex]   can bypass that boundary or the service owns the rule.
[codex] - Do not add guards, fallbacks, custom exception paths, abstractions, or
[codex]   extension points for states that enforced boundaries make impossible.
[codex] - Handle plausible failures explicitly at external and asynchronous boundaries.
[codex] - Before adding a defensive branch, identify how the state can arise and what
[codex]   the caller or system can usefully do in response. Omit the branch if neither
[codex]   is concrete.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Security
[codex] 
[codex] - Every controller endpoint method must have `@PreAuthorize`.
[codex] - The only exception is
[codex]   `InternalPermissionController#getUserPermissions`, which is protected by the
[codex]   narrow `/internal/v1/users/*/permissions` matcher in
[codex]   `PermissionServiceSecurityConfig`. Do not broaden this anonymous
[codex]   claims-header exception; orchestration must continue restricting callers
[codex]   through mesh identity and authorization policy.
[codex] - Use `SecurityContextUtil` to obtain the current user. Do not accept actor
[codex]   identity from a request body or untrusted header in application code.
[codex] - Do not bypass authentication, authorization, persistence, or validation
[codex]   layers as a durable fix.
[codex] - Preserve the deactivation contract: commit the local user-state change before
[codex]   external session revocation, keep transient retry bounded, and keep exhausted
[codex]   revocation safe for the caller to retry. Verify current details in the owner
[codex]   code and configuration before changing this flow.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Roles and Permissions
[codex] 
[codex] - Manage roles, permissions, and role-permission mappings only through Flyway
[codex]   migrations. Do not add a runtime grant surface without an explicit design
[codex]   change.
[codex] - Use `{resource}:{action}` for own-resource permissions and
[codex]   `{resource}:{action}:any` for cross-user scope. Add a scoped permission only
[codex]   when a controller has a real cross-user access requirement; do not pre-create
[codex]   speculative variants.
[codex] - Preserve the grant-time action hierarchy within each scope:
[codex]   - Granting `{resource}:write` also requires `{resource}:read` on the same role.
[codex]   - Granting `{resource}:delete` also requires `{resource}:read` on the same role.
[codex]   - `:write` and `:delete` are independent; neither requires the other.
[codex]   - Before revoking `{resource}:read`, revoke matching `:write` and `:delete` grants.
[codex]   - Apply the same rules independently to `:any` permissions.
[codex] - Do not add runtime permission expansion. Downstream services and UIs rely on
[codex]   literal permission checks against well-formed migration grants.
[codex] - After any migration that adds, grants, or revokes a role permission, remind
[codex]   the user to synchronize
[codex]   `../service-common/service-web/src/main/java/org/budgetanalyzer/service/security/test/ClaimsHeaderTestBuilder.java`.
[codex]   This repository cannot make that required sibling-repository change.
[codex]

[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this

[codex] verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in this repository or
[codex] the shared preset. The app must keep direct, maintained-line, and later-major

[codex] weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] `basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent coverage.
[codex] 
[codex] Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] `GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] `${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The snapshot is submitted directly
[codex] and is not retained as an artifact or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, the pinned
[codex] `service-common` artifacts cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit the internal
[codex] dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Bot pull request checks
[codex] 
[codex] Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring release-train compatibility,
[codex] and whether the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited
[codex] alert. Major updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require
[codex] approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] ./gradlew clean build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add
[codex] `pull_request_target` or expose package credentials to untrusted dependency
[codex] branches to bypass that boundary.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] 
[codex] Phase 4 intentionally used no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials.
[codex] The following checks for `currency-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex]    App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex]    its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex]    Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex]    proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex]    references, and the `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex]    `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex]    uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 4 run
[codex]    extracted all three internal declarations and 57 total occurrences, but
[codex]    reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could not
[codex]    authenticate private Maven lookups. The final revalidation returned 45
[codex]    public candidate branches (an earlier attempt returned 47); both are only
[codex]    partial, time-sensitive evidence.
[codex] 2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex]    `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex]    `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex]    `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex]    must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex]    and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that all three preflight POM
[codex]    requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex]    test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The credential-free
[codex]    Phase 4 snapshot contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero
[codex]    `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring Security, Tomcat, Netty, or PostgreSQL
[codex]    coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable remotely. That
[codex]    partial snapshot is a recorded limitation, not submission proof. Historical
[codex]    build run `34027551741` used the same internal version successfully, but it
[codex]    predates this workflow and is also not submission proof.
[codex] 3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex]    `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex]    is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex]    missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency validation.
[codex]    Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose credentials to an
[codex]    untrusted fork.
[codex] 4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex]    are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex]    requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex]    snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex]    Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, and Netty findings where applicable. An
[codex]    absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has been accepted.
[codex] 
[codex] The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 4
[codex] local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.
[codex]

[codex] # Permission Service
[codex] 
[codex] > "Archetype: service. Role: Manages RBAC and authorization data (roles, permissions, user-role assignments)."
[codex] >
[codex] > — [AGENTS.md](AGENTS.md#tree-position)
[codex] 
[codex] [![Build](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/permission-service/actions/workflows/build.yml/badge.svg)](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/permission-service/actions/workflows/build.yml)
[codex] 
[codex] Authorization data management microservice for the Budget Analyzer application. Manages clean RBAC with 2 default roles (ADMIN, USER), simple join tables for role-permission and user-role mappings, and an internal endpoint [Session Gateway](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/session-gateway) uses during login, token exchange, and heartbeat-driven refresh.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Configuration
[codex] 
[codex] | Property | Value |
[codex] |----------|-------|
[codex] | Port | 8086 |
[codex] | Context Path | `/permission-service` |
[codex] | Database | `permission` |
[codex] | Authentication | Claims headers from Session Gateway / Envoy ext_authz; no local username/password store |
[codex] 
[codex] ## Prerequisites
[codex] 
[codex] - Java 25
[codex] - PostgreSQL database named `permission`
[codex] - OIDC identity provider configured (issuer URI, audience)
[codex] 
[codex] **Local development setup**: See [getting-started.md](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/development/getting-started.md)
[codex] 
[codex] **Service-common artifact resolution**: Local builds resolve `service-common`
[codex] from `mavenLocal()` — no GitHub credentials required. Default GitHub Actions
[codex] `build.yml` runs and release builds resolve the pinned `serviceCommon` version
[codex] from GitHub Packages. The full contract is documented in orchestration:
[codex] [service-common artifact resolution](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration/blob/main/docs/development/service-common-artifact-resolution.md).
[codex] This service imports `org.budgetanalyzer:spring-platform` for shared Spring
[codex] dependency management and keeps `org.budgetanalyzer:service-web` explicit for
[codex] runtime utilities.
[codex] 
[codex] The service intentionally excludes Spring Boot's servlet user-details
[codex] auto-configuration. It does not create or accept generated default passwords;
[codex] request authentication is handled by the shared claims-header security filter
[codex] using headers injected by the trusted gateway path.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Development
[codex] 
[codex] ### Build
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew build
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ### Run
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] cd ../orchestration
[codex] tilt up
[codex] 
[codex] cd ../permission-service
[codex] export SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD=your_permission_database_password
[codex] ./gradlew bootRun
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] `SPRING_DATASOURCE_USERNAME` already defaults to `permission_service`, and the
[codex] host defaults to `localhost:5432`. If you are reusing values from
[codex] `../orchestration/.env`, map `POSTGRES_PERMISSION_SERVICE_PASSWORD` to
[codex] `SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD`.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Test
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] ./gradlew test
[codex] 
[codex] # Generate JaCoCo XML and HTML coverage reports
[codex] ./gradlew test jacocoTestReport
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] Repository integration tests use Testcontainers-backed PostgreSQL, so Docker must be available
[codex] when running the full test suite. `UserRoleRepositoryIntegrationTest` and
[codex] `UserIdentityIntegrationTest` both run Flyway migrations with Hibernate schema validation enabled
[codex] against PostgreSQL rather than the shared H2 test setup.
[codex] 
[codex] Coverage reports are written to `build/reports/jacoco/test/html/index.html` and
[codex] `build/reports/jacoco/test/jacocoTestReport.xml`. `check` enforces the
[codex] configured coverage gates.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Database
[codex] 
[codex] The service uses Flyway for database migrations. Migrations are located in:
[codex] - `src/main/resources/db/migration/`
[codex] 
[codex] ### Default Roles
[codex] 
[codex] Two default roles (ADMIN, USER) are managed exclusively via Flyway migrations, not at runtime. See [docs/authorization-model.md](docs/authorization-model.md) for the full permission matrix, scoped permissions, and action hierarchy rationale.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Capabilities
[codex] 
[codex] **User administration** — Search and view users with their role assignments. Deactivate users, which removes their roles and revokes active sessions.
[codex] 
[codex] **Permission resolution** — Internal endpoint that syncs a user from the identity provider and returns their resolved permissions. Called by Session Gateway during login, token exchange, and session refresh.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Architecture
[codex] 
[codex] See [docs/authorization-model.md](docs/authorization-model.md) for the role/permission data model, rationale, and UI authorization guidance.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Related Repositories
[codex] 
[codex] - [session-gateway](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/session-gateway) — Edge authorization; calls the internal endpoint to sync users and resolve permissions
[codex] - [transaction-service](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/transaction-service) — Transaction management
[codex] - [currency-service](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/currency-service) — Currency management
[codex] - [service-common](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common) — Shared Spring platform and runtime libraries (base entities, exception handling)
[codex] - [orchestration](https://github.com/budgetanalyzer/orchestration) — Infrastructure and local dev environment
[codex]

[codex] # Service-Common Artifact Resolution
[codex] 
[codex] This document explains the split between local `mavenLocal()` resolution and
[codex] the GitHub Packages path used by GitHub Actions and release builds.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Version Scope
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is a shared Java library version, not the deployment version
[codex] for the whole Budget Analyzer stack. Bump and release it when
[codex] `spring-platform`, `spring-cloud-platform`, `service-core`, or `service-web`
[codex] changes. Do not change it only because a service image, frontend artifact,
[codex] route, production overlay, `/api-docs` metadata file, or OCI deployment
[codex] configuration changed.
[codex] 
[codex] Java services can release runtime artifacts while continuing to consume the
[codex] same checked-in `serviceCommon` coordinate from `gradle/libs.versions.toml`.
[codex] The production deployment identity is the digest-pinned image inventory and
[codex] deployment manifest, which may contain mixed service artifact versions.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local Contributor Flow
[codex] 
[codex] The supported side-by-side workspace flow must stay credential-free.
[codex] 
[codex] If you follow [getting-started.md](getting-started.md) and run local
[codex] development through orchestration and Tilt:
[codex] 
[codex] - clone the repos side by side
[codex] - open the sibling `orchestration/` repo
[codex] - run `./setup.sh`
[codex] - run `tilt up`
[codex] 
[codex] you should not need `GITHUB_ACTOR`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, or a personal access token
[codex] just to start the app locally.
[codex] 
[codex] That works because the local build path stays local-first:
[codex] 
[codex] - Java service repos keep `mavenLocal()` first
[codex] - Tilt runs the `service-common-publish` resource first
[codex] - `service-common-publish` runs `publishToMavenLocal`
[codex] - downstream host-side Gradle builds then resolve `service-common` from Maven
[codex]   Local
[codex] 
[codex] If you hit a local `service-common` resolution error while working in the full
[codex] workspace, republish it locally and rerun the service build:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] cd ../service-common
[codex] ./gradlew clean build publishToMavenLocal
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] ## When GitHub Packages Credentials Are Required
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub Packages credentials are a CI/release concern, not a getting-started
[codex] prerequisite.
[codex] 
[codex] You need GitHub Packages credentials when all of these are true:
[codex] 
[codex] - the build is not using the local orchestration/Tilt path
[codex] - `service-common` is not already available through Maven Local
[codex] - the build must resolve the published `org.budgetanalyzer` artifacts remotely
[codex]   from `https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common`
[codex] 
[codex] Typical examples:
[codex] 
[codex] - default GitHub Actions `build.yml` runs in `transaction-service`,
[codex]   `currency-service`, `permission-service`, and `session-gateway`
[codex] - release-image Docker builds
[codex] - isolated CI or clean-builder image builds
[codex] - manual clean-shell verification of remote published artifacts
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` itself now refreshes the current checked-in snapshot from
[codex] `main` through its `publish-snapshot.yml` workflow. That remote snapshot path
[codex] exists to support isolated CI/release builds; it does not change the local
[codex] contributor contract.
[codex] 
[codex] For the Java consumer repos, the default build workflow contract is now:
[codex] 
[codex] - read the pinned `serviceCommon` version from `gradle/libs.versions.toml`
[codex] - fail fast if `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` or
[codex]   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` is missing
[codex] - preflight the versioned `service-core` and `service-web` POM URLs in GitHub
[codex]   Packages
[codex] - run `./gradlew build` with `GITHUB_ACTOR` / `GITHUB_TOKEN` exported from
[codex]   that same secret pair
[codex] 
[codex] ## GitHub Actions Environment
[codex] 
[codex] When a workflow intentionally resolves `service-common` remotely from GitHub
[codex] Packages, Gradle needs both a token and a package username.
[codex] 
[codex] For cross-repo private Maven/Gradle consumption, do not assume the workflow
[codex] repo's own `GITHUB_TOKEN` can read `service-common`. GitHub still treats these
[codex] packages as repository-scoped, so the consuming workflow needs an explicit
[codex] GitHub Packages credential.
[codex] 
[codex] In GitHub Actions, the current repo wiring expects:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] env:
[codex]   GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]   GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] `GITHUB_ACTOR` here means the username that owns the GitHub Packages credential,
[codex] not necessarily `${{ github.actor }}`. `GITHUB_TOKEN` here means the secret used
[codex] for remote package reads, not necessarily the workflow repo's default token.
[codex] This requirement applies to the remote GitHub Packages path used by default
[codex] `build.yml` and release workflows. It does not apply to the normal `tilt up`
[codex] contributor flow.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Containerized Release Builds
[codex] 
[codex] The Java service Dockerfiles now support remote `service-common` resolution for
[codex] release and isolated CI builds without copying host `.m2` state into the build
[codex] context and without checking out sibling source trees.
[codex] 
[codex] Pass the workflow credentials into BuildKit as secrets:
[codex] 
[codex] ```bash
[codex] docker build \
[codex]   --secret id=github_actor,env=GITHUB_ACTOR \
[codex]   --secret id=github_token,env=GITHUB_TOKEN \
[codex]   -f Dockerfile .
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] In GitHub Actions, the release job should still expose those same credential
[codex] values under the env names the current Gradle builds read:
[codex] 
[codex] ```yaml
[codex] env:
[codex]   GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]   GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] ```
[codex] 
[codex] The Dockerfiles read those values only inside the Gradle builder-stage `RUN`
[codex] steps, so the credentials are not written into the image, filesystem layers, or
[codex] checked-in repo files.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Why Public Package Visibility Does Not Remove This
[codex] 
[codex] Making the `service-common` Maven packages public in the GitHub UI does not
[codex] remove the Maven/Gradle authentication requirement.
[codex] 
[codex] Keep the release workflow package-visibility and workflow-access cleanup, but do not
[codex] treat it as the fix for local contributor UX. The fix for local contributor UX
[codex] is keeping the side-by-side workspace and Tilt flow local-first and
[codex] credential-free.
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc "node --version && npm --version && command -v renovate || true && command -v actionlint || true && command -v docker || true && docker info --format '{{.ServerVersion}}' 2>&1 || true && java -version 2>&1 && ./gradlew --version" in /workspace/permission-service
[codex]  succeeded in 254ms:
[codex] 
[codex] ------------------------------------------------------------
[codex] Gradle 9.5.0
[codex] ------------------------------------------------------------
[codex] 
[codex] Build time:    2026-04-28 12:05:30 UTC
[codex] Revision:      3fe117d68f3907790f3809f121aa36303a9151f8
[codex] 
[codex] Kotlin:        2.3.20
[codex] Groovy:        4.0.29
[codex] Ant:           Apache Ant(TM) version 1.10.15 compiled on August 25 2024
[codex] Launcher JVM:  25.0.4.1 (Azul Systems, Inc. 25.0.4.1+1-LTS)
[codex] Daemon JVM:    /usr/lib/jvm/zulu25-ca-amd64 (no Daemon JVM specified, using current Java home)
[codex] OS:            Linux 6.8.0-139-generic amd64
[codex] 
[codex] 
[codex] exec
[codex] /bin/bash -lc "sed -n '526,575p' docs/plans/dependency-automation-plan.md && sed -n '1,360p' docs/dependency-automation.md" in /workspace/orchestration
[codex]  succeeded in 0ms:
[codex] ## Phase 5: Onboard permission-service
[codex] 
[codex] ### Workspace
[codex] 
[codex] ../permission-service
[codex] 
[codex] ### Goal
[codex] 
[codex] Cover this service's declared and resolved dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Scope
[codex] 
[codex] Thin Renovate config, official dependency-submission workflow and local docs.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Non-goals
[codex] 
[codex] No selected-version changes, service code, releases or sibling writes.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Required context
[codex] 
[codex] Read local instructions/catalog/workflows, the shared preset and the artifact
[codex] resolution guide. Reuse the validated Java configuration pattern from Phases 3–4.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Execution steps
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Add the shared-preset config and verify all native dependency managers in use.
[codex] 2. Configure trusted-branch and weekly/manual Gradle graph submission using basic
[codex]    caching, references to existing package-read secrets and a separate submission
[codex]    token. Do not obtain or verify credentials during this phase.
[codex] 3. Document the workflow, local evidence, and this service's own Phase 12
[codex]    runtime/test graph acceptance requirements.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Implementation notes
[codex] 
[codex] Validate repo-specific modules and workflow wiring locally. Credential validation
[codex] and complete remote graph proof belong to Phase 12; another service's successful
[codex] snapshot is not evidence that this service resolves correctly.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Validation
[codex] 
[codex] Run Renovate validation/extraction, credential-free lookup and actionlint. Run
[codex] official local graph generation only where available without credentials; record
[codex] authenticated resolution/build/graph checks as pending Phase 12. Preserve pins.
[codex] 
[codex] ### Completion criteria
[codex] 
[codex] The repo has independently validated local bot/workflow configuration, extraction
[codex] evidence, and explicit Phase 12 handoffs for authenticated checks. Complete remote
[codex] graph evidence and credential review are not required to complete preparation.
[codex] 
[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] **Status:** Configuration prepared; administrator activation is still required.
[codex] 
[codex] This document owns the operating policy for dependency automation across the
[codex] Budget Analyzer repositories. The preserved
[codex] [September 6 dependency review](research/dependency-update-review-2026-09-06.md)
[codex] is the historical acceptance benchmark; its observed versions are not bot
[codex] targets. The executable rollout and acceptance sequence remains in the
[codex] [dependency automation plan](plans/dependency-automation-plan.md).
[codex] 
[codex] ## Ownership and policy
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate is the only service allowed to open dependency update pull requests.
[codex] Dependabot remains alert-only: keep the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] enabled, disable Dependabot version updates and overlapping Dependabot security
[codex] update pull requests, and let Renovate consume alerts when its App permission
[codex] allows that. Never enable automerge, including for security fixes.
[codex] 
[codex] The shared policy is
[codex] [`renovate-presets/default.json`](../renovate-presets/default.json). Repository
[codex] configuration extends it and adds only repository-specific manager paths and
[codex] extraction rules. Routine pull requests are created weekly, with no more than
[codex] three open routine pull requests and two new routine pull requests per hour in
[codex] one repository. Vulnerability fixes use Renovate's dedicated alert path and an
[codex] unrestricted schedule. Major, chart, stateful, platform, and checksum-coupled
[codex] updates require Dependency Dashboard approval.
[codex] 
[codex] Patch, minor, and major proposals remain distinct. Digest refreshes must retain
[codex] an immutable digest and any image flavor suffix. Reviewers must independently
[codex] verify Linux ARM64 support. An available tag or a changed digest is not evidence
[codex] of architecture support, embedded package versions, vulnerability remediation,
[codex] or upstream support status.
[codex] 
[codex] The following stay outside Renovate ownership:
[codex] 
[codex] - historical/generated outputs and the retained stale test suites
[codex] - local `:latest` and generated `:tilt-*` images listed by the image-pinning
[codex]   inventories
[codex] - first-party production images and their synchronized manifest, inventory,
[codex]   overlay, runtime metadata, and API-docs metadata
[codex] - Pod Security compatibility labels
[codex] - arbitrary per-platform checksum calculation
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate may propose checksum-coupled tool versions, but dashboard approval and
[codex] the existing checksum verification remain mandatory. A reviewer must update the
[codex] complete checksum table; no validation may be weakened to merge the proposal.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Cost and service boundary
[codex] 
[codex] Use the open-source Renovate engine through the free Mend Renovate Community
[codex] GitHub App. Do not provision a self-hosted bot or opt into paid Merge Confidence,
[codex] enterprise scheduling, enhanced cache, a trial, or another subscription. The
[codex] selected security evidence uses GitHub's dependency graph and Dependabot alerts,
[codex] Gradle's open-source basic dependency-submission path, Trivy, `npm audit`, and
[codex] `govulncheck`.
[codex] 
[codex] GitHub Actions usage is separate from Mend hosting. Public repositories can use
[codex] standard hosted Linux runners. Before enabling automation for a private
[codex] repository, confirm its included Actions allowance and that paid overages are
[codex] disabled. Stop for user direction if required coverage needs spending or a
[codex] capability unavailable on the free offering.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Activation procedure
[codex] 
[codex] Configuration preparation does not authorize GitHub setting changes. A
[codex] repository administrator performs activation only after all rollout phases have
[codex] prepared and locally validated their files. Phases 1–11 require no credentials;
[codex] all authenticated validation and credential review belong to Phase 12. Agents
[codex] must never receive GitHub, package, registry, or Mend credentials, including in
[codex] Phase 12. The user performs authenticated operations in GitHub/Mend or their own
[codex] trusted environment and supplies sanitized evidence for agent review.
[codex] 
[codex] Missing authentication, authenticated-only API access, and unauthenticated API
[codex] rate limits are recorded as pending Phase 12 evidence; they do not block local
[codex] configuration preparation. This includes private Maven resolution, complete
[codex] remotely resolved Java snapshots, graph submission, published-preset resolution,
[codex] and hosted bot/scanner runs. Local schema validation, extraction, workflow lint,
[codex] and checks that can run without credentials remain required. Preserve actual
[codex] failures and incomplete outputs; never report a deferred check as passed or
[codex] weaken a hosted job's failure behavior. Non-authentication prerequisites and
[codex] implementation defects remain blockers under the owning repository's rules.
[codex] 
[codex] For each deferred check, record the repository, check/command, affected packages
[codex] or configurations, observed failure or reason not attempted, and the Phase 12
[codex] operator action and expected proof. Sibling phases keep this handoff in their
[codex] own docs and link here; Phase 12 consolidates it in the coverage report.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 12 operator sequence is:
[codex] 
[codex] 1. Confirm repository visibility, Actions billing/overage settings, and that the
[codex]    free Mend service supports every scoped repository and required authenticated
[codex]    Maven lookup within its job limits. Complete this before publication can
[codex]    trigger any prepared push workflows.
[codex] 2. Publish the orchestration repository and its shared preset before publishing
[codex]    consumer configurations.
[codex] 3. Manually dispatch
[codex]    `dependency-automation-config.yml` with `run_hosted_dry_run` enabled. Its
[codex]    GitHub-provided job token has read permissions only, Renovate runs with
[codex]    `--dry-run=full`, and the user retains control of the trigger. Preserve the
[codex]    workflow URL and confirm that the published preset resolves before proceeding;
[codex]    never copy the job token into an agent environment.
[codex] 4. Install the Renovate Community App only on the repositories listed in the
[codex]    rollout plan. Grant read access to Dependabot alerts when available.
[codex] 5. Enable the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts. Disable Dependabot version
[codex]    updates and automatic Dependabot security-update pull requests.
[codex] 6. Configure private Maven access only through Mend-supported encrypted secrets
[codex]    and host rules. Never commit a token and never use `pull_request_target` to
[codex]    expose trusted credentials to dependency branches.
[codex] 7. Run each Java repository's graph-submission workflow on its trusted default
[codex]    branch using its existing scoped package-read secrets and a separate GitHub
[codex]    job token for submission. Verify complete application/runtime/test dependency
[codex]    coverage and GitHub acceptance for every repo. A successful ordinary build,
[codex]    package-access preflight, or partial snapshot is insufficient. Preserve a
[codex]    sanitized snapshot or equivalent detailed graph evidence with the run URL and
[codex]    source revision; the orchestration-only Renovate dry run does not prove Maven
[codex]    resolution or Java submission. Resolve every other deferred authenticated
[codex]    lookup/build/scan and retain its evidence too.
[codex] 8. Run onboarding for orchestration first. Inspect the resolved configuration,
[codex]    extraction logs, dashboard, job duration, and any rate-limit or timeout
[codex]    message before enabling consumers.
[codex] 9. Record every baseline item in the coverage report as reproduced, superseded,
[codex]    false positive with evidence, or missing. Update discovery, advisory evidence,
[codex]    and lifecycle assessment are separate results.
[codex] 
[codex] The Community service's documented single concurrent organization job,
[codex] approximately four-hour scheduling, and 30-minute timeout are acceptable only if
[codex] the pilot completes in practice. A timeout or unsupported authenticated lookup
[codex] blocks broader activation; it is not a reason to omit dependencies.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Local validation and dry run
[codex] 
[codex] The focused workflow validates both JSON files with Renovate's official
[codex] `renovate-config-validator` under Node 24. Before publishing the preset, run the
[codex] same strict schema validation locally. For an unpublished-preset dry run, load
[codex] `renovate-presets/default.json` as the local self-hosted configuration, suppress
[codex] repository config discovery, and process the checkout with the local platform.
[codex] Use debug logging and do not provide a GitHub token or enable a write-capable
[codex] platform mode. Local mode proves extraction and supported lookups but does not
[codex] perform a true full dry run. Phase 12 owns the post-publication GitHub-platform
[codex] full dry run through the manually dispatched, read-only workflow; its ephemeral
[codex] job token is never provided to an agent.
[codex] 
[codex] Extraction and lookup evidence belongs in
[codex] [`docs/research/dependency-automation-coverage.md`](research/dependency-automation-coverage.md),
[codex] not in a second desired-version inventory.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Exact-image security evidence
[codex] 
[codex] `exact-image-security-evidence.yml` runs weekly and by manual dispatch. It has
[codex] no pull-request trigger and findings do not create a merge gate. The job fails
[codex] when rendering, registry resolution, database download, package inventory, or a
[codex] vulnerability scan is incomplete; vulnerability findings themselves leave the
[codex] scan successful. The final upload runs even after failure and retains evidence
[codex] for seven days without relying on SARIF hosting or a paid service.
[codex] 
[codex] The workflow calls `scripts/security/render-image-scan-inputs.sh`, which uses
[codex] the production Kustomize overlays, controller chart versions from
[codex] `deploy/scripts/lib/version-contract.sh`, their checked-in values, and the
[codex] Prometheus/Kiali post-renderers. It includes Helm-created controllers and hooks,
[codex] operator-supplied cert-manager solver and Prometheus config-reloader images,
[codex] standalone Jaeger and infrastructure images, and the Tilt Temurin, Kind, and
[codex] frontend production-smoke images. First-party service images remain under the
[codex] release workflow and are excluded from this scanner job.
[codex] 
[codex] Production/controller images are resolved and scanned as `linux/arm64`.
[codex] Local-only sources use the actual scanner-runner platform (`linux/amd64` on the
[codex] hosted job). For each deduplicated target, the artifact contains:
[codex] 
[codex] - the rendered source ref and every file that contributed it
[codex] - the registry manifest and the selected platform child digest
[codex] - a Trivy all-package JSON inventory with OS and application package classes
[codex] - a separate JSON vulnerability report plus captured stderr
[codex] - Trivy CLI/database and registry-resolver metadata, database-download logs,
[codex]   and a machine-readable completion status
[codex] 
[codex] A rendered tag without a digest is labelled `mutable` even though the job
[codex] immediately resolves and scans one exact platform digest. This preserves the
[codex] difference between repeatable evidence for that run and immutable checked-in
[codex] desired state. In particular, External Secrets chart 2.2.0 currently renders
[codex] `ghcr.io/external-secrets/external-secrets:v2.2.0`; its checked-in `image.digest`
[codex] value is not emitted by that chart version. The tag resolved to the intended
[codex] digest during Phase 2 validation, but later tag movement remains possible.
[codex] The cert-manager controller/startup refs and Istio CNI/pilot refs are likewise
[codex] tag-only chart output; only their resolved artifact for a particular run is
[codex] exact. The cert-manager ACME solver input does retain its checked-in digest.
[codex] 
[codex] The existing `deploy/scripts/render/observability.sh` and
[codex] `guardrails/verify-production-image-overlay.sh` are not CI render entry points:
[codex] they intentionally use a live server-side Helm dry run for Kiali RBAC. The
[codex] scanner invokes ordinary `helm template` with the same Kiali values and
[codex] post-renderer, so it needs no Kubernetes API or production credentials. One
[codex] known gap remains explicit: the Istio gateway chart renders `image: auto`, and
[codex] only a live Istio injector chooses the proxy image. The workflow records that
[codex] target as a known unscannable gap rather than adding a live-cluster dependency
[codex] or claiming coverage. Unexpected render, resolution, database, or scan failures
[codex] still fail the job.
[codex] 
[codex] Trivy package evidence is not a lifecycle authority and does not guarantee that
[codex] an image exposes its primary application version. The coverage report records
[codex] known Redis, Temurin, and controller-binary inventory limitations. Never infer a
[codex] Redis patch or Java CPU level from the broad source tag or from a successful OS
[codex] package scan.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Failure triage
[codex] 
[codex] - **Configuration error:** reproduce with the pinned validator version. Treat
[codex]   warnings and migrations as failures; correct the preset or repository config.
[codex] - **No extraction:** check the owning file's native manager pattern or adjacent
[codex]   annotation and compare debug logs. Do not seed the historical expected version
[codex]   into configuration.
[codex] - **No lookup or wrong package:** verify the real datasource identity, chart
[codex]   name, registry URL, tag prefix, build suffix, and flavor suffix. Record an
[codex]   unresolved standard-tool gap instead of adding a custom crawler.
[codex] - **Authentication or resolution failure:** preserve the failure. Fix encrypted
[codex]   Maven/registry access through the Phase 12 operator handoff. During Phases 1–11,
[codex]   record authentication-dependent checks as pending and continue independent local
[codex]   work. Never seek credentials in the agent environment, use Maven Local as proof
[codex]   of remote resolution, drop internal dependencies, or swallow the error. A
[codex]   resolution defect unrelated to authentication still needs investigation.
[codex] - **Finding without an update:** retain the Dependabot/scanner finding and route
[codex]   inherited or transitive remediation to the owning service. An update pull
[codex]   request is not proof that an advisory was detected.
[codex] - **CI failure on a bot branch:** determine whether the proposal needs its
[codex]   documented checksum, chart rendering, ARM64, state migration, or cross-repo
[codex]   companion review. Do not relax a guardrail or create orchestration drift.
[codex] - **Scanner database/download failure:** report the job as failed separately
[codex]   from vulnerability findings. Never turn an unavailable scan into a clean result.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate discovery and vulnerability scanners do not comprehensively establish
[codex] end-of-life status. Owners must still perform a quarterly support-policy review
[codex] against upstream lifecycle sources.
[codex]

[codex] 1576:[codex] 1856:[codex] [codex] # The `dependency-submission` action
[codex] 1577:[codex] 1858:[codex] [codex] The `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action provides the simplest (and recommended) way to generate a 
[codex] 1578:[codex] 1862:[codex] [codex] The dependency graph snapshot is generated via integration with the [GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin](https://plugins.gradle.org/plugin/org.gradle.github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin), and submitted to your repository via the

[codex] 2571:[codex] [codex] - `DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_REPORT_DIR` (optional): Specifies where the dependency graph report will be generated. Must be an absolute path.
[codex] 2575:[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_NAME`: Sets the `detector.name` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to `GitHub Dependency Graph Gradle Plugin`
[codex] 2576:[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_VERSION`: Sets the `detector.version` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to current version of the plugin.
[codex] 2577:[codex] [codex] - `GITHUB_DEPENDENCY_GRAPH_DETECTOR_URL`: Sets the `detector.url` value for the dependency submission. Defaults to `https://github.com/gradle/github-dependency-graph-gradle-plugin`

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +

[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +

[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}
