[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644

[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +

[codex] +

[codex] +

[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,123 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,70 @@
[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 extraction and lookup results are recorded after the repository
[codex] +configuration is validated. Authenticated GitHub Actions and private Maven
[codex] +lookups remain hosted Phase 12 checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json

[codex] +failure triage are owned by

[codex] +checks.

[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot recorded below is a limitation, not submission proof.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +name: Dependency Submission
[codex] +
[codex] +on:
[codex] +  push:
[codex] +    branches: [main]
[codex] +  schedule:
[codex] +    - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex] +  workflow_dispatch:
[codex] +
[codex] +permissions: {}
[codex] +
[codex] +env:
[codex] +  FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] +
[codex] +jobs:
[codex] +  submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex] +    if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex] +    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex] +    permissions:
[codex] +      contents: write
[codex] +
[codex] +    steps:
[codex] +      - name: Checkout code
[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +
[codex] +          for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 5 local extraction found 45 dependency occurrences across eight
[codex] +files: 25 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three
[codex] +Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows.
[codex] +Public Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced 45 candidate branches at the
[codex] +time of validation. GitHub Actions lookups and the three
[codex] +`org.budgetanalyzer` Maven catalog coordinates remain authenticated hosted
[codex] +checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read token, so those checks
[codex] +are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 validation ran the official GitHub dependency graph Gradle plugin
[codex] +without submitting its output. The generated snapshot contained 87 build/tool
[codex] +coordinates and eight direct entries, but no `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring
[codex] +Security, Tomcat, Netty, PostgreSQL, Testcontainers, or WireMock coordinates.
[codex] +The accompanying `runtimeClasspath` report marked `spring-platform`,
[codex] +`service-web`, and every dependent application runtime declaration as failed to
[codex] +resolve because the credential-free environment could not access the pinned
[codex] +private artifacts. This partial snapshot proves only that local generation ran;
[codex] +it is not application, runtime, test, remote-resolution, or submission evidence.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 5 run
[codex] +   extracted all three internal catalog declarations and 45 total occurrences,
[codex] +   but reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could
[codex] +   not authenticate private Maven lookups. Its 45 public candidate branches are
[codex] +   partial, time-sensitive evidence only.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot is a limitation, not submission proof. The Phase 5 snapshot
[codex] +   contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero internal, application
[codex] +   runtime, or test coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable
[codex] +   without authenticated remote resolution.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 5 local extraction found 45 dependency occurrences across eight
[codex] +files: 25 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three
[codex] +Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows.
[codex] +Public Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced 45 candidate branches at the
[codex] +time of validation. GitHub Actions lookups and the three
[codex] +`org.budgetanalyzer` Maven catalog coordinates remain authenticated hosted
[codex] +checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read token, so those checks
[codex] +are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 validation ran the official GitHub dependency graph Gradle plugin
[codex] +without submitting its output. The generated snapshot contained 87 build/tool
[codex] +coordinates and eight direct entries, but no `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring
[codex] +Security, Tomcat, Netty, PostgreSQL, Testcontainers, or WireMock coordinates.
[codex] +The accompanying `runtimeClasspath` report marked `spring-platform`,
[codex] +`service-web`, and every dependent application runtime declaration as failed to
[codex] +resolve because the credential-free environment could not access the pinned
[codex] +private artifacts. This partial snapshot proves only that local generation ran;
[codex] +it is not application, runtime, test, remote-resolution, or submission evidence.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 5 run
[codex] +   extracted all three internal catalog declarations and 45 total occurrences,
[codex] +   but reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could
[codex] +   not authenticate private Maven lookups. Its 45 public candidate branches are
[codex] +   partial, time-sensitive evidence only.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot is a limitation, not submission proof. The Phase 5 snapshot
[codex] +   contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero internal, application
[codex] +   runtime, or test coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable
[codex] +   without authenticated remote resolution.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.

[codex] FILE .github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml
[codex] name: Dependency Submission
[codex] 
[codex] on:
[codex]   push:
[codex]     branches: [main]
[codex]   schedule:
[codex]     - cron: '37 5 * * 1'
[codex]   workflow_dispatch:
[codex] 
[codex] permissions: {}
[codex] 
[codex] env:
[codex]   FORCE_JAVASCRIPT_ACTIONS_TO_NODE24: 'true'
[codex] 
[codex] jobs:
[codex]   submit-gradle-dependencies:
[codex]     if: github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
[codex]     runs-on: ubuntu-latest
[codex]     permissions:
[codex]       contents: write
[codex] 
[codex]     steps:
[codex]       - name: Checkout code
[codex]         uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex]         with:
[codex]           persist-credentials: false
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Set up JDK 25
[codex]         uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex]         with:
[codex]           java-version: '25'
[codex]           distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Verify service-common package access
[codex]         shell: bash
[codex]         env:
[codex]           PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]           PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex]         run: |
[codex]           set -euo pipefail
[codex] 
[codex]           if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           service_common_version="$(sed -n 's/^serviceCommon = "\([^"]*\)"$/\1/p' gradle/libs.versions.toml)"
[codex]           if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex]             echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex]             exit 1
[codex]           fi
[codex] 
[codex]           for artifact in spring-platform service-web; do
[codex]             pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex]             curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex]               --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex]               --output /dev/null \
[codex]               "${pom_url}"
[codex]           done
[codex] 
[codex]       - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex]         uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex]         env:
[codex]           GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex]           GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex]         with:
[codex]           cache-provider: basic
[codex]           dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex]           additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex]           github-token: ${{ github.token }}
[codex] FILE docs/dependency-automation.md
[codex] # Dependency Automation
[codex] 
[codex] The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] failure triage are owned by
[codex] [orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] checks.
[codex] 
[codex] ## Update discovery
[codex] 
[codex] `renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] `serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] 
[codex] `service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] supports them.
[codex] 
[codex] The Phase 5 local extraction found 45 dependency occurrences across eight
[codex] files: 25 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three
[codex] Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows.
[codex] Public Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced 45 candidate branches at the
[codex] time of validation. GitHub Actions lookups and the three
[codex] `org.budgetanalyzer` Maven catalog coordinates remain authenticated hosted
[codex] checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read token, so those checks
[codex] are pending rather than passed.
[codex] 
[codex] Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex]

[codex] +        uses: actions/checkout@v6
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          persist-credentials: false
[codex] +

[codex] +        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          java-version: '25'
[codex] +          distribution: 'temurin'
[codex] +

[codex] +        shell: bash
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          PACKAGES_USERNAME: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        run: |
[codex] +          set -euo pipefail
[codex] +
[codex] +          if [[ -z "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}" || -z "${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Missing SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME or SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +

[codex] +          if [[ -z "${service_common_version}" ]]; then
[codex] +            echo "Could not read serviceCommon from gradle/libs.versions.toml." >&2
[codex] +            exit 1
[codex] +          fi
[codex] +

[codex] +            pom_url="https://maven.pkg.github.com/budgetanalyzer/service-common/org/budgetanalyzer/${artifact}/${service_common_version}/${artifact}-${service_common_version}.pom"
[codex] +            curl --fail --silent --show-error \
[codex] +              --user "${PACKAGES_USERNAME}:${PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN}" \
[codex] +              --output /dev/null \
[codex] +              "${pom_url}"
[codex] +          done
[codex] +
[codex] +      - name: Generate and submit dependency graph
[codex] +        uses: gradle/actions/dependency-submission@v6
[codex] +        env:
[codex] +          GITHUB_ACTOR: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME }}
[codex] +          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN }}
[codex] +        with:
[codex] +          cache-provider: basic
[codex] +          dependency-graph: generate-and-submit
[codex] +          additional-arguments: --no-configuration-cache
[codex] +          github-token: ${{ github.token }}

[codex] @@ -0,0 +1,145 @@
[codex] +# Dependency Automation
[codex] +
[codex] +The workspace-wide operating policy, activation steps, cost boundary, and
[codex] +failure triage are owned by
[codex] +[orchestration's dependency automation guide](../../orchestration/docs/dependency-automation.md).
[codex] +This document records only the `permission-service` integration and review
[codex] +checks.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Update discovery
[codex] +
[codex] +`renovate.json` extends the shared Budget Analyzer preset. Renovate's native
[codex] +Gradle, Gradle Wrapper, Dockerfile, and GitHub Actions managers discover this
[codex] +repository's version catalog, build script, wrapper distribution, base images,
[codex] +and workflow actions. The Gradle catalog extraction includes the declared
[codex] +`serviceCommon` version used by `spring-platform` and `service-web`.
[codex] +
[codex] +`service-common` is hosted in GitHub Packages. Extraction does not prove that
[codex] +Renovate can look up those private Maven coordinates. Before activation,
[codex] +configure the Mend Renovate Community App's supported encrypted Maven
[codex] +credentials and verify an authenticated lookup; never put a package token in
[codex] +this repository or the shared preset. The app must keep direct,
[codex] +maintained-line, and later-major proposals visible where each datasource
[codex] +supports them.
[codex] +
[codex] +The Phase 5 local extraction found 45 dependency occurrences across eight
[codex] +files: 25 Gradle occurrences in three files, one wrapper occurrence, three
[codex] +Dockerfile occurrences, and 16 GitHub Actions occurrences in three workflows.
[codex] +Public Gradle, wrapper, and image lookups produced 45 candidate branches at the
[codex] +time of validation. GitHub Actions lookups and the three
[codex] +`org.budgetanalyzer` Maven catalog coordinates remain authenticated hosted
[codex] +checks; the local runner had no GitHub or package-read token, so those checks
[codex] +are pending rather than passed.
[codex] +
[codex] +Renovate proposes changes only to direct declarations. Versions inherited from
[codex] +the Spring Boot and `spring-platform` BOMs remain represented by the resolved
[codex] +dependency graph described below. A direct Spring Boot or `serviceCommon`
[codex] +proposal is not proof that every inherited vulnerability is fixed.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Resolved dependency graph
[codex] +
[codex] +`.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml` runs on trusted pushes to `main`,
[codex] +weekly, and by manual dispatch. The job runs only for the `main` ref and uses
[codex] +the official `gradle/actions/dependency-submission` action with the open-source
[codex] +`basic` cache provider. It resolves all projects and all resolvable
[codex] +configurations so application, runtime, build, and test dependency trees are
[codex] +included. Do not add configuration filters without proving equivalent
[codex] +coverage.
[codex] +
[codex] +Remote resolution uses `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +`SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN`, exposed to Gradle as `GITHUB_ACTOR` and
[codex] +`GITHUB_TOKEN`. These package-read credentials are distinct from
[codex] +`${{ github.token }}`, which the action uses to submit the graph with the job's
[codex] +only elevated permission, `contents: write`. The package-access preflight
[codex] +checks the exact `spring-platform` and `service-web` artifacts used by this
[codex] +service. The snapshot is submitted directly and is not retained as an artifact
[codex] +or published as a Build Scan.
[codex] +
[codex] +The workflow must fail when package credentials are missing, either pinned
[codex] +`service-common` artifact cannot be resolved, graph generation is incomplete,
[codex] +or GitHub rejects submission. Do not substitute Maven Local, omit an internal
[codex] +dependency, filter failed configurations, or treat graph-generation failure as
[codex] +a clean security result. A hosted run on `main` is the final proof that GitHub
[codex] +accepted the submission and can produce Dependabot alerts from it.
[codex] +
[codex] +Local Phase 5 validation ran the official GitHub dependency graph Gradle plugin
[codex] +without submitting its output. The generated snapshot contained 87 build/tool
[codex] +coordinates and eight direct entries, but no `org.budgetanalyzer`, Spring
[codex] +Security, Tomcat, Netty, PostgreSQL, Testcontainers, or WireMock coordinates.
[codex] +The accompanying `runtimeClasspath` report marked `spring-platform`,
[codex] +`service-web`, and every dependent application runtime declaration as failed to
[codex] +resolve because the credential-free environment could not access the pinned
[codex] +private artifacts. This partial snapshot proves only that local generation ran;
[codex] +it is not application, runtime, test, remote-resolution, or submission evidence.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Bot pull request checks
[codex] +
[codex] +Every Renovate pull request remains non-automerged. Review the resolved
[codex] +dependency diff, release notes, Java 25 and Spring compatibility, and whether
[codex] +the proposed direct dependency actually remediates any inherited alert. Major
[codex] +updates remain visible in the Dependency Dashboard and require approval.
[codex] +
[codex] +Run the required repository validation in order:
[codex] +
[codex] +```bash
[codex] +./gradlew clean spotlessApply
[codex] +./gradlew clean build
[codex] +```
[codex] +
[codex] +Bot pull requests use the existing `build.yml` pull-request workflow. Forked or
[codex] +otherwise untrusted pull requests do not receive package-read secrets, so a
[codex] +failure to resolve `service-common` there is an unavailable-secret condition,
[codex] +not evidence that the dependency is absent. Do not add `pull_request_target` or
[codex] +expose package credentials to untrusted dependency branches to bypass that
[codex] +boundary.
[codex] +
[codex] +## Phase 12 authenticated evidence handoff
[codex] +
[codex] +Phase 5 intentionally uses no GitHub, GitHub Packages, or Mend credentials. The
[codex] +following checks for `permission-service` remain pending Phase 12 and must be
[codex] +performed by the operator in the trusted hosted environments named below:
[codex] +
[codex] +1. Publish the orchestration shared preset, install the Mend Renovate Community
[codex] +   App for this repository, grant its required alert-read access, and configure
[codex] +   its supported encrypted Maven host rule for GitHub Packages. Run the hosted
[codex] +   Renovate job and retain its log or Dependency Dashboard URL. The expected
[codex] +   proof is successful resolution of the published preset, GitHub Actions
[codex] +   references, and all `org.budgetanalyzer` declarations in
[codex] +   `gradle/libs.versions.toml`, with no authentication, configuration, timeout,
[codex] +   uncaught-exception, or lookup failure. The credential-free Phase 5 run
[codex] +   extracted all three internal catalog declarations and 45 total occurrences,
[codex] +   but reported that a GitHub token was required for Actions lookups and could
[codex] +   not authenticate private Maven lookups. Its 45 public candidate branches are
[codex] +   partial, time-sensitive evidence only.
[codex] +2. From the trusted `main` branch, dispatch or observe
[codex] +   `.github/workflows/dependency-submission.yml`. The operator must leave
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_USERNAME` and
[codex] +   `SERVICE_COMMON_PACKAGES_READ_TOKEN` in the repository's secret store and
[codex] +   must not expose their values to an agent. Retain the successful workflow URL
[codex] +   and GitHub dependency-graph snapshot as proof that both preflight POM
[codex] +   requests succeeded, Gradle remotely resolved the application, runtime, and
[codex] +   test configurations, and GitHub accepted the submission. The accepted graph
[codex] +   must include `spring-platform`, `service-web`, the Spring Boot web, security,
[codex] +   validation, and JPA stacks, Flyway and PostgreSQL, and the H2,
[codex] +   Testcontainers, WireMock, Spring Security, and JUnit test trees. Any local
[codex] +   partial snapshot is a limitation, not submission proof. The Phase 5 snapshot
[codex] +   contained only 87 build/tool coordinates and zero internal, application
[codex] +   runtime, or test coordinates because `serviceCommon` 0.0.16 was unavailable
[codex] +   without authenticated remote resolution.
[codex] +3. On an actual Renovate pull request, retain the `build.yml` run that executes
[codex] +   `./gradlew build` with the existing package-read secrets. The expected proof
[codex] +   is either a successful build or a specific dependency compatibility failure;
[codex] +   missing-secret or package-authentication output is not dependency
[codex] +   validation. Do not move this check to `pull_request_target` or disclose
[codex] +   credentials to an untrusted fork.
[codex] +4. In GitHub administration, confirm the dependency graph and Dependabot alerts
[codex] +   are enabled while overlapping version-update and security-update pull
[codex] +   requests remain disabled. After the hosted submission, retain the accepted
[codex] +   snapshot and resulting alert URLs, including inherited Spring Framework,
[codex] +   Spring Security, Jackson, Tomcat, PostgreSQL, and Netty findings where
[codex] +   applicable. An absent alert is meaningful only after the complete graph has
[codex] +   been accepted.
[codex] +
[codex] +The orchestration coverage report owns the final cross-repository acceptance
[codex] +record. Phase 12 must copy these URLs and any explained misses there; no Phase 5
[codex] +local result activates automation or establishes vulnerability-review parity.
[codex] diff --git a/renovate.json b/renovate.json
[codex] new file mode 100644
[codex] index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..320bbbd1585ee13f76f1f2253ba91b919bc4e568
[codex] --- /dev/null

[codex] +  ]
